Keeping this\n  # calculation shared ensures the public encounter endpoints cannot drift.\n  def summed_thresholds(party)\n    totals = { easy: 0, medium: 0, hard: 0, deadly: 0 }\n    party.each do |member|\n      level = integer(member[\"level\"]) if member.is_a?(Hash)\n      values = THRESHOLDS[level]\n      return nil unless values\n\n      totals.each_key { |key| totals[key] += values[key] }\n    end\n    totals\n  end\n\n  def encounter_multiplier(count)\n    return 1 if count == 1\n    return 1.5 if count == 2\n    return 2 if count <= 6\n    return 2.5 if count <= 10\n    return 3 if count <= 14\n\n    4\n  end\n\n  def difficulty_for(xp, thresholds)\n    return \"deadly\" if xp >= thresholds[:deadly]\n    return \"hard\" if xp >= thresholds[:hard]\n    return \"medium\" if xp >= thresholds[:medium]\n    return \"easy\" if xp >= thresholds[:easy]\n\n    \"trivial\"\n  end\nend\n\nclass EncountersController < ApplicationController\n  include EncounterMath\n\n  def adjusted_xp\n    body = json_body\n    party = body[\"party\"]\n    monsters = body[\"monsters\"]\n    return bad_request unless party.is_a?(Array) && monsters.is_a?(Array)\n\n    thresholds = summed_thresholds(party)\n    return bad_request unless thresholds\n\n    base_xp = 0\n    monster_count = 0\n    monsters.each do |monster|\n      return bad_request unless monster.is_a?(Hash) && XP.key?(monster[\"cr\"].to_s)\n\n      count = integer(monster[\"count\"])\n      return bad_request unless count&.positive?\n\n      base_xp += XP.fetch(monster[\"cr\"].to_s) * count\n InitiativeController < ApplicationController\n  def order\n    combatants = json_body[\"combatants\"]\n    return bad_request unless combatants.is_a?(Array)\n\n    order = combatants.map do proficiency\n    level = valid_level(json_body[\"level\"])\n    return bad_request unless level\n\n    render json: { level: level, proficiency_bonus: proficiency_for(level) }\n  end\n\n  def derived_stats\n    body = json_body\n    level = valid_level(body[\"level\"])\n    modifiers = modifiers_for(body[\"abilities\"])\n    armor = body[\"armor\"]\n    return bad_request unless level && score&.between?(1, 30)\n  end\n\n  def valid_level(value)\n    level = integer(value)\n    level if level&.between?(1, 20)\n  end\n\n  def ability_modifier_for(score)\n    (score - 10).div(2)\n  end\n\n  def proficiency_for(level)\n    2 + ((level - 1) / 4)\n  end\n\n  def modifiers_for(abilities)\n    return nil unless abilities.is_a?(Hash) && abilities.keys.sort == ABILITIES.sort\n\n    ABILITIES.to_h do |ability|\n      score = valid_score(abilities[ability])\n      return nil unless score\n\n      [ability, ability_modifier_for(score)]\n    end\n  end\nend\n\nmodule CombatSessionState\n  private\n\n  def initiative_order(combatants)\n    return nil unless combatants.is_a?(Array) && combatants.any?\n\n    order = combatants.map do |combatant|\n      return nil unless combatant.is_a?(Hash) && combatant[\"name\"].is_a?(String) && !combatant[\"name\"].empty?\n\n      dex = integer(combatant[\"dex\"])\n      roll = integer(combatant[\"roll\"])\n      return nil if dex.nil? || roll.nil?\n\n      { name: combatant[\"name\"], score: roll + dex, dex: dex }\n    end\n    return nil unless order.map { |combatant| combatant[:name] }.uniq.length == order.length\n\n    order.sort_by { |combatant| [-combatant[:score], -combatant[:dex], combatant[:name]] }\n  end\n\n  def decrement_conditions(entries)\n    return unless entries\n\n    entries.each { |entry| entry[:remaining_rounds] -= 1 }\n    entries.reject! { |entry| entry[:remaining_rounds] <= 0 }\n  end\n\n  def session_state(id, session, include_order: false, include_conditions: false)\n    active = session[:order][session[:turn_index]].slice(:name, :score)\n    state = { id: id, round: session[:round], turn_index: session[:turn_index], active: active }\n    state[:order] = session[:order].map { |combatant| combatant.slice(:name, :score) } if include_order\n    if include_conditions\n      state[:conditions] = session[:conditions].each_with_object({}) do |(name, entries), result|\n        # Keep the target present after its last condition expires.  Consumers\n        # can then distinguish a known combatant with no current conditions\n        # from one that has never had a condition attached.\n        result[name] = entries.map(&:dup)\n      end\n    end\n    state\n  end\n\n  def not_found\n    render json: { error: \"unknown session\" }, status: :not_found\n  end\nend\n\nmodule AuthCredentials\n  USERNAME = /\\A[a-z0-9_-]{2,32}\\z/\n  ROLES = %w[dm player].freeze\n  ITERATIONS = 100_000\n  KEY_LENGTH = 32\n\n  private\n\n  def valid_registration?(username, password, role)\n    username.is_a?(String) && USERNAME.match?(username) &&\n      password.is_a?(String) && password.length >= 8 && ROLES.include?(role)\n  end\n\n  def password_record(password)\n    salt = SecureRandom.random_bytes(16)\n    { salt: salt, digest: password_digest(password, salt) }\n  end\n\n  def password_matches?(password, user)\n    ActiveSupport::SecurityUtils.secure_compare(password_digest(password, user[:salt]), user[:digest])\n  end\n\n  def password_digest(password, salt)\n    OpenSSL::PKCS5.pbkdf2_hmac(password, salt, ITERATIONS, KEY_LENGTH, \"sha256\")\n  end\n\n  def unauthorized\n    render json: { error: \"bad credentials\" }, status: :unauthorized\n  end\nend\n\n# Play tokens are deterministic and therefore remain valid across a storage\n# reset. Registered accounts are also accepted, which keeps the auth API\n# usable for callers that create their own actors.\nmodule PlayAuthentication\n  FIXED_ACTOR_ROLES = {\n    \"dm\" => \"dm\",\n    \"player\" => \"player\",\n    \"player-a\" => \"player\",\n    \"player-b\" => \"player\",\n    \"stranger\" => \"player\"\n  }.freeze\n\n  private\n\n  def current_play_actor\n    authorization = request.authorization\n    match = /\\ABearer session-([a-z0-9_-]{2,32})\\z/.match(authorization.to_s)\n    return nil unless match\n\n    registered_actor = GameStorage.synchronize do\n      GameStorage.database.get_first_row(\n        \"SELECT username, role FROM users WHERE username = ?\", [match[1]]\n      )\n    end\n    return registered_actor if registered_actor\n\n    role = FIXED_ACTOR_ROLES[match[1]]\n    role && { \"username\" => match[1], \"role\" => role }\n  end\n\n  def require_play_actor\n    actor = current_play_actor\n    return actor if actor\n\n    render json: { error: \"bad credentials\" }, status: :unauthorized\n    nil\n  end\nend\n\n# Persistent controller; its state transition helpers live in CombatSessionState.\nclass CombatSessionsController < ApplicationController\n  include CombatSessionState\n\n  def create\n    body = json_body\n    id = body[\"id\"]\n    order = initiative_order(body[\"combatants\"])\n    return bad_request unless id.is_a?(String) && !id.empty? codebase\\n\\n## Start and verify\\n\\nUse the pinned bundle and provide a port:\\n\\n```sh\\nPORT=3000 ./run.sh\\n```\\n\\n`run.sh` replaces its shell with Puma through `rackup`, binds it to\\n`127.0.0.1`, and keeps the server in the foreground.  In another shell, a\\nminimal smoke check is:\\n\\n```sh\\ncurl --fail http://127.0.0.1:3000/health\\n```\\n\\nThe response is `{\\\"ok\\\":true}`.  Before submitting a change, at minimum run\\n`ruby -c app.rb`; run the cumulative benchmark evaluator when it is available\\nin the surrounding benchmark environment.  Do not start a long-running server\\nas part of static verification.\\n\\n## Entry points and layout\\n\\nThis intentionally minimal Rails API is kept in a small number of root files:\\n\\n- `run.sh` is the server command used by the benchmark.\\n- `config.ru` requires `app.rb` and exposes `Rails.application` to Rack.\\n- `app.rb` defines the Rails application, controllers, shared domain helpers,\\n  SQLite storage, and all routes.  It loads only the Rails controller stack;\\n  Active Record and Rails migrations are deliberately not used.\\n- `Gemfile` and `Gemfile.lock` pin the Rails/Rack/Puma runtime.\\n\\n## Request routing and state\\n\\n`DndApi.routes` at the end of `app.rb` is the authoritative route list.\\nControllers inherit from `ApplicationController`, which parses request JSON,\\nnormalizes integer inputs, and renders the common invalid-request response.\\n\\n`GameStorage` owns the SQLite connection, schema setup, reset operation, and a\\nprocess-local mutex.  Its database file is `game.db` beside `app.rb` and\\npersists users, combat sessions, compendium records, and campaign records\\nacross server restarts.  `GameStorage.initialize_schema!` runs during boot.\\nAll storage reads and writes are made inside `GameStorage.synchronize` so the\\nsingle shared SQLite connection is used safely by Puma threads.  Storage reset\\nis intentionally exposed through `POST /v1/storage/reset` and is the only API\\noperation that clears persisted state.\\n\\n## API/domain groupings\\n\\n- Core rules: health, dice statistics, ability checks, initiative ordering,\\n  characters, and adjusted encounter XP.\\n- Combat and auth: persistent combat sessions/conditions/turn advancement and\\n  SQLite-backed user registration/login.  `CombatSessionState` and\\n  `AuthCredentials` hold their non-routing mechanics.\\n- Compendium and campaigns: monster/item records, campaign characters/events,\\n  persistent scheduled sessions/attendance, campaign state, and deterministic\\n  analytics summaries/risk reports.\\n- PHB utilities: spell slots, long-rest recovery, and equipment load.\\n- DM tools: encounter building from campaign/compendium data, deterministic\\n  tier-one loot, and the latest campaign-event recap.  `EncounterMath` is\\n  shared by the core and DM encounter endpoints to keep their XP behavior in\\n  lockstep.\\n\\n## Safe extension and testing conventions\\n\\nPreserve the existing route, status-code, response-shape, validation, and\\npersistence contracts: the benchmark is cumulative.  Put controller-only\\nrequest handling in the relevant controller and extract only genuinely shared,\\nside-effect-free logic into a focused module.  Keep database access inside the\\nstorage mutex and use parameterized SQL.  Add schema changes to\\n`GameStorage.initialize_schema!` and ensure `reset!` drops any new tables.\\n\\nPrefer deterministic ordering and values; existing APIs intentionally sort\\ninitiative and query campaign collections by insertion order. codebase\\n\\n## Start and verify\\n\\nUse the pinned bundle and provide a port:\\n\\n```sh\\nPORT=3000 ./run.sh\\n```\\n\\n`run.sh` replaces its shell with Puma through `rackup`, binds it to\\n`127.0.0.1`, and keeps the server in the foreground.  In another shell, a\\nminimal smoke check is:\\n\\n```sh\\ncurl --fail http://127.0.0.1:3000/health\\n```\\n\\nThe response is `{\\\"ok\\\":true}`.  Before submitting a change, at minimum run\\n`ruby -c app.rb`; run the cumulative benchmark evaluator when it is available\\nin the surrounding benchmark environment.  Do not start a long-running server\\nas part of static verification.\\n\\n## Entry points and layout\\n\\nThis intentionally minimal Rails API is kept in a small number of root files:\\n\\n- `run.sh` is the server command used by the benchmark.\\n- `config.ru` requires `app.rb` and exposes `Rails.application` to Rack.\\n- `app.rb` defines the Rails application, controllers, shared domain helpers,\\n  SQLite storage, and all routes.  It loads only the Rails controller stack;\\n  Active Record and Rails migrations are deliberately not used.\\n- `Gemfile` and `Gemfile.lock` pin the Rails/Rack/Puma runtime.\\n\\n## Request routing and state\\n\\n`DndApi.routes` at the end of `app.rb` is the authoritative route list.\\nControllers inherit from `ApplicationController`, which parses request JSON,\\nnormalizes integer inputs, and renders the common invalid-request response.\\n\\n`GameStorage` owns the SQLite connection, schema setup, reset operation, and a\\nprocess-local mutex.  Its database file is `game.db` beside `app.rb` and\\npersists users, combat sessions, compendium records, and campaign records\\nacross server restarts.  `GameStorage.initialize_schema!` runs during boot.\\nAll storage reads and writes are made inside `GameStorage.synchronize` so the\\nsingle shared SQLite connection is used safely by Puma threads.  Storage reset\\nis intentionally exposed through `POST /v1/storage/reset` and is the only API\\noperation that clears persisted state.\\n\\n## API/domain groupings\\n\\n- Core rules: health, dice statistics, ability checks, initiative ordering,\\n  characters, and adjusted encounter XP.\\n- Combat and auth: persistent combat sessions/conditions/turn advancement and\\n  SQLite-backed user registration/login.  `CombatSessionState` and\\n  `AuthCredentials` hold their non-routing mechanics.\\n- Compendium and campaigns: monster/item records, campaign characters/events,\\n  persistent scheduled sessions/attendance, campaign state, and deterministic\\n  analytics summaries/risk reports.\\n- PHB utilities: spell slots, long-rest recovery, and equipment load.\\n- DM tools: encounter building from campaign/compendium data, deterministic\\n  tier-one loot, and the latest campaign-event recap.  `EncounterMath` is\\n  shared by the core and DM encounter endpoints to keep their XP behavior in\\n  lockstep.\\n\\n## Safe extension and testing conventions\\n\\nPreserve the existing route, status-code, response-shape, validation, and\\npersistence contracts: the benchmark is cumulative.  Put controller-only\\nrequest handling in the relevant controller and extract only genuinely shared,\\nside-effect-free logic into a focused module.  Keep database access inside the\\nstorage mutex and use parameterized SQL.  Add schema changes to\\n`GameStorage.initialize_schema!` and ensure `reset!` drops any new tables.\\n\\nPrefer deterministic ordering and values; existing APIs intentionally sort\\ninitiative and query campaign collections by insertion order.
